# Seed 50 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - small: hypervolume +0.4% (Iteration 296)

`seed 50 tag2 12h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier --cpus
2,8`, commit 45efa6254 (Iteration 288: the same genes as seeds 48 and 49, a third
time), started 13:49 UTC Oct 14; the evolution 13:53-23:56 (10 h 03 min, the owner's
12 hours less the comparison's), the front measured again by 00:22, the comparison
packed by 02:01. 47,033 designs measured (46,744 alive), 14,337 screened.

The comparison's own verdict (five measures): fastest -0.1%, smallest +0.16%, memory
-2 KB, hypervolume +0.4%, best Q +0.2% - **small**, the gene set's third run. The
Q-best design of all runs is this run's ed821c2edc: 0.156 at 28,243 bytes, 160 KB (Q
0.216 of s6's). Calibration 0.999 on cpu 2, 1.004 on cpu 8.

From the run's database (lab/evolve/seed-facts.py, and the front's genomes):

- **Deaths: 289, all "reach limit at scale 0 - not run".**
- The screen: 14,337 of the 59,760 children from generation 5 skipped (24.0%).
- Children: mutation 53.4%, crossover 35.2%, borrowed 11.4%. Via the attic: 9,814
  children; 4 of the front.
- The final front, 187 designs: mutation 153, borrowed 19, crossover 11, carried 4.
- The small third (62 designs, 15,647-18,516 bytes): fsys 3 on 40, 5 on 15; klookup
  on 2. The fast third (25,305-36,233): fsys 2 on 61; klookup on 48.
- Seed 48's genes: factor on 174 of the 187 (level 3 on 121); lto on 117 of the 159
  GCC-built; clang on 28; jitk on 48; gotodup on 24.
- The JIT on 109 of the front (jittail on 70).
- In the run: fastest e23b0e4770 0.129 at 35,490 (196 KB); smallest 4c2b4e4485
  15,647 (speed 1.481, 128 KB) - 25 bytes under seed 49's; least memory 99b6de9488,
  116 KB (16,573 bytes).

# Evolved VM designs

47033 designs evaluated, 46744 alive, 14337 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 9d798997d9, speed 0.148, binary + image 28,050, memory 160 KB - Q 0.198 of s6-cv8b's.

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
